Action: upgrade VS Code to latest stable (e.g., 1.105.1).\n- Git on SERVER-2016 (2.21.0) — multiple critical CVEs (e.g., CVE-2019-1353, CVE-2019-1387, CVE-2019-19604)\n  - Submodule update and WSL/NTFS protections issues. Action: upgrade Git (Windows) to latest (2.50.1): https://github.com/git-for-windows/git/releases/latest\n- Microsoft Edge 88.0.705.81 on BB-WIN8-1-64 — multiple Chromium-based critical CVEs (CVE-2021-30606, 30607, 30608, 30610–30624, 21157, etc.)\n  - Action: update Edge to a current build (auto-update channel) or remove if not required.\n- Legacy PHP 5.4.16 on Linux (bb-amazonlinux2.local and other RHEL-family hosts) — numerous critical CVEs (WDDX, exif, mbstring, zip, gd/libgd, mcrypt, session unserialize)\n  - Action: decommission PHP 5.4.x. Upgrade to a supported PHP (8.x) or remove. If not feasible, isolate service, put behind WAF, and backport vendor patches where available.\n\nTargeted examples (from the dataset)\n- python 2.7.15/2.7.17/3.7.9/3.8.10 across Windows servers: CVE-2007-4559, CVE-2019-10160, CVE-2019-9636, CVE-2019-9948, CVE-2021-3177\n- debian-opswat-vm / BB-debian11: apt/python-related CVEs including CVE-2021-29921, CVE-2020-27619\n- bb-ubuntu22.localdomain: Visual Studio Code 1.25.1 CVEs (update to 1.105.1)\n- SERVER-2016: Git 2.21.0 CVEs (update to 2.50.1)\n\nRecommended next steps\n- Quick wins (broad impact):\n  - Patch Python across Windows fleet to current LTS (to address CVE-2007-4559, 2021-3177, etc.).\n  - Update Git to latest (2.50.1) on SERVER-2016.\n  - Update Visual Studio Code to latest on Linux desktops/servers.\n  - Replace or upgrade PHP 5.4.x services (or isolate if upgrade is not immediately possible).\n  - Update Microsoft Edge on legacy machines (or remove if unused).\n- Prioritize by asset criticality:\n  - Identify critical business systems among the 15 machines hit by CVE-2007-4559; patch first.\n  - Do a per-machine breakdown to create patch waves with maintenance windows.\n\nWould you like me to:\n- Export a CSV of all vulnerabilities (by machines or by applications)?\n- Provide a per-machine vulnerability breakdown to plan patch waves?\n- Create filtered lists (e.g., all Critical on Windows servers, or all Python-related CVEs)?\n- Open remediation tasks with recommended package versions per host? | YES |

| Tool\_Add\_Sensor\_To\_Group | Add sensor 'sensor-999' to sensor group 'group-111' | Move sensors 'sensor-123' and 'sensor-456' to 'Production' group | Assign sensor 'sensor-new' to 'Development' sensor group |
| Tool\_Remove\_Sensor\_From\_Group | Remove sensor 'sensor-222' from its current sensor group | Take sensor 'sensor-456' out of 'Testing' group | Remove multiple sensors from 'Temporary' group |
| Tool\_Delete\_Sensor\_Groups | Delete sensor group 'old-group-333' and move all sensors to default group | Remove unused sensor group 'deprecated-group' from system | Delete sensor group 'test-group' and reassign sensors |
| Tool\_Scan\_Sensor | Start a full malware scan on sensor 'sensor-scan-444' | Run quick scan on all sensors in 'Production' group | Stop all running scans on sensors in 'Test Lab' group |
| Tool\_Create\_Sensor\_Policy | Create a new sensor policy named 'Strict Security Policy' with Anti-Malware enabled | Build sensor policy 'Standard Protection' with balanced security settings | Create policy 'High Security' with aggressive malware detection |
| Tool\_Get\_Sensors\_Policies | Get list of all available sensor policies | Show all configured sensor policies with their settings | Retrieve sensor policy inventory from the system |
| Tool\_Get\_Sensor\_Policy\_Details | Get complete configuration details for sensor policy 'policy-555' | Show full settings for sensor policy 'Standard Protection' | Retrieve detailed configuration of policy 'High Security' |
| Tool\_Assign\_Policy\_To\_Sensor | Assign policy 'policy-666' to sensors 'sensor-111' and 'sensor-222' without keeping manual overrides | Apply 'Strict Security Policy' to all sensors in 'Finance' group | Assign policy 'Standard Protection' to sensor 'sensor-new' with manual overrides |
| Tool\_Get\_Isolation\_Rule | Get all machine isolation exception rules currently configured | List all isolation rules allowing specific communications | Show isolation exception rules for management traffic |
| Tool\_Create\_Isolation\_Rule | Create isolation exception rule allowing isolated machines to communicate with 10.0.0.5 on port 443 | Allow isolated machines to reach management server 10.10.10.1 on port 8443 | Create rule permitting isolated systems to access DNS server 10.0.1.5 on port 53 |
| Tool\_Update\_Isolation\_Rule | Update isolation rule 'rule-777' to allow bidirectional traffic on port 8080 | Modify rule 'rule-555' to change port from 443 to 8443 | Update isolation exception 'rule-333' to block instead of allow |
| Tool\_Delete\_Isolation\_Rule | Delete isolation exception rule 'rule-888' | Remove obsolete isolation rule 'rule-666' | Delete unused exception rule 'rule-444' from configuration |
| Tool\_Create\_L2\_Responder\_User | Create L2 Responder user 'ir.responder@company.com' with two-factor authentication enabled | Add new L2 Responder 'incident.handler@company.com' with TFA required | Create user 'soc.analyst@company.com' with Responder L2 role and SSO login |
| Tool\_Upload\_And\_Deploy\_Tool\_Package | Upload and deploy incident response tool package 'forensic-collector.exe' for Windows 64-bit systems | Deploy IR tool 'memory-dumper.zip' to Windows sensors with EXECUTE permissions | Upload tool package 'log-collector' and deploy to all Windows servers |
| Tool\_Monitor\_Tool\_Deployment | Monitor deployment status of IR tool package 'forensic-collector' on sensor 'sensor-123' | Check deployment progress of tool 'memory-dumper' across Production group | Monitor IR tool 'log-collector' deployment status on all targeted sensors |
| Tool\_Get\_List\_Tool\_Packages | Get list of all uploaded incident response tool packages | Show all IR tools available in the environment | Retrieve inventory of deployed incident response tools |
| Tool\_Run\_Incident\_Response\_Tool | Run incident response tool 'forensic-collector' on sensors in 'Production' group with custom output directory | Execute IR tool 'memory-dumper' on sensor 'sensor-urgent' with specific parameters | Run tool 'log-collector' on compromised machines with elevated privileges |
| Tool\_Monitor\_Incident\_Response\_Tool | Monitor execution status of incident response tool with batch ID 'ir-batch-456' | Check progress of IR tool run with batch 'ir-batch-789' | Monitor tool execution batch 'ir-batch-123' for completion status |
| Tool\_Load\_Results\_For\_Incident\_Response\_Tool | Load results from incident response tool 'forensic-collector' execution to GCP bucket | Retrieve IR tool 'memory-dumper' results and upload to storage | Load completed results from tool 'log-collector' to GCP bucket |
| Tool\_Delete\_Tool\_Package | Delete incident response tool package 'old-forensic-tool' | Remove obsolete IR tool 'deprecated-collector' from platform | Delete unused tool package 'legacy-dumper' and remove from endpoints |
| Tool\_Get\_Credentials\_GCP\_Bucket | Retrieve GCP bucket credentials for accessing tool results | Get access credentials for IR tool results storage bucket | Fetch GCP bucket authentication details for results download |
| Tool\_Get\_Forensics\_Ingestion\_Tools | Get list of all supported forensic data ingestion tools | Show available forensic tools like KAPE and Velociraptor | Retrieve supported forensic data collection tools |
| Tool\_Deploy\_Forensics\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tool | Deploy forensic data ingestion tool 'KAPE' for Windows 64-bit systems | Deploy 'Velociraptor' forensic tool to all Windows servers | Install forensic tool 'CyLR' on sensors in Incident Response group |
| Tool\_Monitor\_Deployment\_Forensic\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tools | Monitor deployment of forensic tool 'KAPE' on sensor 'sensor-789' | Check deployment status of 'Velociraptor' across targeted machines | Monitor forensic tool 'CyLR' deployment progress on all sensors |
| Tool\_Run\_Forensic\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tool | Run forensic data ingestion tool 'KAPE' on machines between January 1-15, 2024 | Execute 'Velociraptor' forensic collection on compromised systems from last 48 hours | Run forensic tool 'CyLR' on specific machines for time range December 2024 |
| Tool\_Run\_Forensic\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tool\_CSV | Run forensic tool 'Velociraptor' using sensors from uploaded CSV file | Execute 'KAPE' collection on machines listed in CSV import | Run forensic tool 'CyLR' on sensors specified in uploaded CSV file |
| Tool\_Monitor\_Forensic\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tool\_Execution | Monitor execution of forensic data ingestion tool with batch ID 'forensic-batch-999' | Check progress of forensic collection batch 'forensic-batch-888' | Monitor forensic tool run status for batch 'forensic-batch-777' |
| Tool\_Delete\_Forensic\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tool | Delete forensic data ingestion tool 'KAPE' from all endpoints | Remove 'Velociraptor' forensic tool from sensors | Delete forensic tool 'CyLR' from all machines in environment |
| Tool\_List\_Users | Get list of all Cybereason platform users | NaN | NaN |
| Tool\_Create\_User | Create a new user with the following details:\n- user\_name: test\_agent\n- password: StrongPass123!\n- roles: ["analyst\_l1"] | NaN | NaN |
| Tool\_Update\_User | Update user 'test\_agent to add system admin role | Modify user 'analyst@company.com' to enable two-factor authentication | Update user 'lead@company.com' roles and change password requirements |
| Tool\_Delete\_User | Delete user account 'old.user@company.com' | Remove terminated employee 'former.analyst@company.com' | Delete unused user account 'test.user@company.com' |
| Tool\_Retrieve\_User\_Audit\_Logs | Retrieve audit logs of all user activities in the platform | Get user activity logs for the last 30 days | Show audit trail of all user actions and changes |

Server."\n  },\n  "NotSupported": {\n    "type": "integer",\n    "description": "The number of Sensors that are running a Sensor version that is not supported."\n  },\n  "Primed": {\n    "type": "integer",\n    "description": "The number of Sensors ready for the action to be sent."\n  },\n  "ChunksRequired": {\n    "type": "integer",\n    "description": "The number of Sensors that need the next parts of the information to be sent."\n  },\n  "ProbeRemoved": {\n    "type": "integer",\n    "description": "The number of Sensors that had a probe removed from the machine."\n  },\n  "Started": ============================================================================\n# SHARED SENSOR FILTER SCHEMA - Use across all sensor-related tools\n# ============================================================================\n\nSENSOR\_FILTER\_ITEM = {\n    "type": "object",\n    "properties": {\n        "fieldName": {\n            "type": "string",\n            "enum": [\n                # Integer/Numeric Fields\n                "actionsInProgress", "avDbLastUpdateTime", "cpuUsage", "decommissionedSensors",\n                "decommissionTimestamp", "memoryUsage", "purgeTimestamp", "siteId", "staleTimeMS", \n                "upTime",\n                \n                # Timestamp Fields\n                "archiveTimeMs", "deliveryTime", "disconnectionTime", "firstSeenTime", \n                "lastFullScheduleScanSuccessTime", "lastQuickScheduleScanSuccessTime",\n                "lastPylumUpdateTimestampMs", "offlineTimeMS", "onlineTimeMS", "sensorLastUpdate",\n                "statusTimeMS",\n                \n                # String Fields\n                "amModeOrigin", "antiMalwareModeOrigin", "archivedOrUnarchiveComment", \n                "avDbVersion", "collectiveUuid", "consoleVersion", "customTags", "DeletedBy",\n                "DeletedDate", "department", "deviceType", "deviceModel", "decommissionedByUser",\n                "exitReason", "externalIpAddress", "fqdn", "groupId", "groupName", "guid",\n                "HeartBeatWin", "internalIpAddress", "lastStatusAction", "location", "machineName",\n                "organization", "organizationalUnit", "policyId", "policyName", "preventionError",\n                "privateServerIp", "proxyAddress", "pylumID", "sensorId", "sensorArchivedByUser",\n                "sensorPurgedByUser", "serialNumber", "serverId", "serverIp", "serverName",\n                "siteName", "version",\n                \n                # Boolean Fields\n                "compliance", "criticalAsset", "disconnected", "groupStickiness", "isolated",\n                "outdated", "purgedSensors",\n                \n                # Enum Fields - Anti-Malware\n                "amStatus", "antiMalwareStatus", "serviceStatus", "staticAnalysisDetectMode",\n                "staticAnalysisDetectModeOrigin", "staticAnalysisPreventMode", \n                "staticAnalysisPreventModeOrigin",\n                \n                # Enum Fields - Protection Features\n                "antiExploitStatus", "bepmode", "documentProtectionStatus", "documentProtectionMode",\n                "fwStatus", "powerShellStatus", "preventionStatus", "ransomwareStatus",\n                "remoteShellStatus", "usbStatus", "variantFilePreventionMode", "vppMode",\n                \n                # Enum Fields - Collection & Status\n                "collectionComponents", "collectionStatus", "fullScanStatus", "quickScanStatus",\n                "groupStickinessLabel", "status",\n                \n                # Enum Fields - Operating System\n                "osType", "osVersionType",\n                \n                # Enum Fields - Upgrade Status\n                "lastUpgradeResult", "lastUpgradeSteps",\n                \n                # Array Fields\n                "pendingActions"\n            ],\n            "description": "The sensor attribute field to filter on"\n        },\n        "operator": {\n            "type": "string",\n            "enum": [\n                "Equals",\n                "NotEquals",\n                "ContainsIgnoreCase",\n                "NotContainsIgnoreCase",\n                "LessThan",\n                "LessOrEqualsTo",\n                "GreaterThan",\n                "GreaterOrEqualsTo",\n                "Between"\n            ],\n            "description": "The comparison operator to use. String fields: Contains operators. Enum/Boolean fields: Equals operators. Numeric/Timestamp fields: Comparison operators"\n        },\n        "values": {\n            "type": "array",\n            "items": {\n                "type": ["string", "integer", "boolean", "number"]\n            },\n            "description": "The value(s) to filter by. Type depends on fieldName"\n        }\n    },\n    "required": ["fieldName", "operator", "values"]\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# OPERATOR USAGE GUIDE BY FIELD TYPE\n# ============================================================================\n\nOPERATOR\_GUIDE = {\n    # String Fields - Use Contains operators\n    "string\_fields": {\n        "operators": ["ContainsIgnoreCase", "NotContainsIgnoreCase", "Equals", "NotEquals"],\n        "examples": ["machineName", "fqdn", "department", "location"]\n    },\n    \n    # Enum Fields - Use Equals operators\n    "enum\_fields": {\n        "operators": ["Equals", "NotEquals"],\n        "examples": ["status", "osType", "collectionStatus", "ransomwareStatus"]\n    },\n    \n    # Boolean Fields - Use Equals operators\n    "boolean\_fields": {\n        "operators": ["Equals"],\n        "examples": ["isolated", "outdated", "compliance", "criticalAsset"]\n    },\n    \n    # Numeric/Timestamp Fields - Use Comparison operators\n    "numeric\_timestamp\_fields": {\n        "operators": ["Equals", "NotEquals", "LessThan", "LessOrEqualsTo", "GreaterThan", "GreaterOrEqualsTo", "Between"],\n        "examples": ["cpuUsage", "memoryUsage", "firstSeenTime", "disconnectionTime"]\n    }\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# ENUM VALUES FOR COMMON FILTER FIELDS\n# ============================================================================\n\nENUM\_VALUES = {\n    "status": ["Online", "Offline", "Stale", "Archived"],\n    "osType": ["UNKNOWN\_OS", "WINDOWS", "OSX", "LINUX"],\n    "collectionStatus": ["ENABLED", "DISABLED", "SUSPENDED"],\n    "ransomwareStatus": ["UNKNOWN", "DISABLED", "DETECT\_ONLY", "DETECT\_AND\_SUSPEND", "DETECT\_SUSPEND\_PREVENT"],\n    "preventionStatus": ["ENABLE", "DISABLE", "UNINSTALL", "UNKNOWN"],\n    "powerShellStatus": ["PS\_DISABLED", "PS\_ENABLED", "PS\_DEFAULT"],\n    "groupStickinessLabel": ["Manual", "Dynamic"],\n    "amStatus": ["AM\_INSTALLED", "AM\_UNINSTALLED"],\n    "antiExploitStatus": ["ENABLED", "DISABLED"],\n    "fwStatus": ["DISABLED", "ENABLED"],\n    "usbStatus": ["ENABLED", "DISABLED"],\n    "remoteShellStatus": ["AC\_DISABLED", "AC\_ENABLED"],\n    "bepmode": ["DISABLED", "DETECT", "PREVENT"],\n    "vppMode": ["DISABLED", "DETECT", "PREVENT"],\n    "documentProtectionStatus": ["DS\_UNKNOWN", "DS\_DISABLED", "DS\_DETECT", "DS\_PREVENT", "DS\_QUARANTINE"],\n    "documentProtectionMode": ["DM\_UNKNOWN", "DM\_CAUTIOUS", "DM\_MODERATE", "DM\_AGGRESSIVE"],\n    "staticAnalysisDetectMode": ["DISABLED", "CAUTIOUS", "MODERATE", "AGGRESSIVE", "SET\_BY\_POLICY"],\n    "staticAnalysisPreventMode": ["DISABLED", "CAUTIOUS", "MODERATE", "AGGRESSIVE"],\n    "variantFilePreventionMode": ["VFP\_MODE\_DISABLED", "VFP\_MODE\_DETECT", "VFP\_MODE\_PREVENT", "VFP\_MODE\_QUARANTINE"],\n    "serviceStatus": ["DISABLED", "DETECT", "PREVENT", "SET\_BY\_POLICY"]\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# USAGE IN TOOLS - Reference this shared schema\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get\_sensor\_query\_parameters():\n    """\n    Example: Query Sensors tool parameters\n    """\n    return {\n        "type": "object",\n        "properties": {\n            "limit": {\n                "type": "integer",\n                "description": "Number of sensors to return (max 30,000)",\n                "maximum": 30000\n            },\n            "offset": {\n                "type": "integer",\n                "description": "Starting position for pagination",\n                "default": 0\n            },\n            "sortDirection": {\n                "type": "string",\n                "enum": ["ASC", "DESC"],\n                "description": "Sort order"\n            },\n            "filters": {\n                "type": "array",\n                "description": "Array of filter criteria to select sensors",\n                "items": SENSOR\_FILTER\_ITEM  # ← Reference shared schema\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\ndef get\_sensor\_action\_parameters():\n    """\n    Example: Sensor action tools (Start Collection, Restart, etc.)\n    """\n    return {\n        "type": "object",\n        "properties": {\n            "sensorsIds": {\n                "type": "array",\n                "items": {"type": "string"},\n                "description": "Specific sensor IDs (use this OR filters)"\n            },\n            "filters": {\n                "type": "array",\n                "description": "Filter criteria to select sensors (use this OR sensorsIds)",\n                "items": SENSOR\_FILTER\_ITEM  # ← Reference shared schema\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TOOLS THAT USE THIS SHARED FILTER\n# ============================================================================\n\nTOOLS\_USING\_SENSOR\_FILTERS = [\n    "Tool\_Query\_Sensors",\n    "Tool\_File\_Search",\n    "Tool\_Start\_Collection",\n    "Tool\_Stop\_Collection",\n    "Tool\_Restart\_Sensor",\n    "Tool\_Upgrade\_Sensor",\n    "Tool\_Set\_Ransomware\_Mode",\n    "Tool\_Set\_Application\_Control\_Mode",\n    "Tool\_Set\_Anti\_Malware\_Mode",\n    "Tool\_Set\_Powershell\_Protection\_Mode",\n    "Tool\_Scan\_Sensor",\n    "Tool\_Monitor\_Tool\_Deployment",\n    "Tool\_Run\_Incident\_Response\_Tool",\n    "Tool\_Load\_Results\_For\_Incident\_Response\_Tool",\n    "Tool\_Deploy\_Forensics\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tool",\n    "Tool\_Monitor\_Deployment\_Forensic\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tools",\n    "Tool\_Run\_Forensic\_Data\_Ingestion\_Tool"\n]
